Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in parsing theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 319 and the invariant I(A,B) = 204 mod 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_319(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 319. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^204(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Statement: Let the parsing structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 65. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 845 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 65 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the parsing structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 845. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
